100集剧本逐集核对

2026-11-11 · 原稿100集 / 分镜900个 · 待用户逐集确认

100集均出现床、枕头或被褥；100个末镜使用同一卧室安睡镜位。口播中的月亮类词出现于26集，星字出现于6集，窗出现于70集。主要问题是空间、事件和收尾模板重复。

每集展开后可看原九镜、具体问题和讨论候选。候选没有写回剧本；未开展新媒体制作。

第001集 · 小兔子皮皮的晚安星光　保留为单集入睡特例

原场景：已验收的小木屋卧室及窗外｜原主题：在熟悉的陪伴里放松与入睡

原稿问题：第1镜已经靠枕盖毯，第2/5/7镜看月星树叶；适合作为一次陪睡开篇，缺少事件，不宜成为100集通用模板。

讨论方向，未批准：保留这一集的卧室陪伴功能；后续集切换地点、人物目标与结尾，不沿用它的床窗构图。

相近集数：002、007、086、090、097、100

镜01 · 小屋与小灯

夜深了，森林里的小屋，亮着一盏暖暖的小灯。 小兔子皮皮靠在枕头上，盖着他的小毛毯。

镜位：中景建立环境，主体位置清楚

原提示词：Use case: illustration-story. Asset type: the first full-bleed vertical 9:16 frame and visual reference for a gentle preschool bedtime story, no panels. Create a beautiful refined hand-painted watercolor children's picture-book illustration with visible subtle paper grain, rounded simple shapes and soft edges, muted dusty blue, sage and cream, warm restrained amber light, peaceful quiet atmosphere. Scene: inside a small cozy wooden forest cottage bedroom at night. Composition: small wooden bed on the left/lower middle, wooden window in the upper right showing a crescent moon and a dark quiet leafy forest. A little shaded bedside lamp casts soft warm light. Main character Pip is a small adorable soft gray bunny, round face, tiny pink nose, long relaxed ears, wearing a deep navy drooping nightcap dotted with a few tiny pale yellow stars. Pip rests comfortably against a cream pillow with sleepy half-open eyes, a warm yellow blanket pulled over his body. A small simple orange carrot plush with muted green leaves rests near his paws. Keep the room uncluttered, use confident storybook composition, gentle contrast without harsh highlights, hand-drawn facial features, not photorealistic and not 3D. The bunny should be clearly readable at phone size. Leave a quiet low-detail area near the lower edge for possible later captions. Full-bleed single image, absolutely no text, letters, labels, borders, collages or watermark. Portrait 9:16, ideally 1080x1920 or larger.

镜02 · 月亮与树叶

窗外，月亮弯弯的，树叶轻轻摇。

镜位：主体或物件近景，保留地点线索

原提示词：Use case: illustration-story. Asset: one new full-bleed 9:16 vertical illustration for scene 02 of the SAME gentle bedtime picture book. Reference image 1 supplies the established cottage bedroom, watercolor technique, wooden window, cream curtains printed with muted olive leaves, deep blue forest, warm amber and dusty blue palette. Create a closer view of this SAME wooden crossbar window seen from inside the room. The crescent moon is visible near the upper right, quiet blue forest and a few soft leafy branches outside. Curtains frame both sides. A very small edge of the cream pillow and yellow blanket may appear at lower left, but NO bunny is required. Focus on the calm moon and softly resting leaves to match the narration about moon and leaves. The special golden star has not appeared yet: do not add a prominent five-point star. Soft warm lamplight touches the lower window sill. Exact same refined hand-painted watercolor and paper grain as reference, muted colors and gentle contrast, no realism, no 3D, no new props. Single image, no panels, text, border, labels or watermark. Portrait 9:16.

镜03 · 哈欠与放松

原提示词：Use case: illustration-story. Input image 1 is the approved visual reference for character, room, watercolor technique and palette. Create ONE new full-bleed portrait 9:16 shot for scene 02 of the same bedtime picture book. Preserve precisely the gray bunny Pip's round face, pink nose, long soft floppy ears, deep navy drooping nightcap with pale yellow stars, warm yellow blanket, cream pillow, cute orange carrot plush with green leaves, wooden bed, leafy curtains, warm lamp and blue night window. Change composition to a closer medium shot of Pip from chest up in the same bed. His eyes are half closed and his little mouth forms a gentle tiny yawn, relaxed and adorable, no dramatic expression. His paws rest on the blanket pulled up to his chin, carrot plush beside one paw. Do not add hands or limbs. Keep enough of the window in the upper right and warm bedside light from lower left to recognize the same room. Muted peaceful soft hand-painted watercolor and paper texture, no dramatic contrast, no photorealism, no 3D. Single illustration, not a collage, no text, no borders, no watermark. Portrait 9:16.

The window fills the upper two-thirds, its wooden crossbars, cream leafy curtains and deep blue forest are consistent with the reference. The same small crescent moon remains near the upper right. Add one modest softly glowing five-point pale golden star just to the LEFT of that crescent moon; it is small and gentle, no flare, no trail. In the lower left foreground show only the back and a little side profile of the SAME sleepy gray bunny with the navy yellow-star nightcap and long floppy ear, looking up toward the star while still tucked under the yellow blanket, not standing. A little warm light from the bedside lamp below the frame. Preserve the hand-painted watercolor paper texture, subdued cozy amber/blue palette, simple rounded shapes. Quiet breathing room and elegant composition, no bright white star or sparkling particle effects. No text, border, collage or watermark, vertical 9:16.

His eyes are nearly closed, a tiny peaceful smile. Keep gray fur, round face, small pink nose, long soft floppy ears and EXACT same deep navy star-patterned nightcap with yellow pom-pom. Medium close composition, slightly wider than the yawning closeup. Show the cream pillow and curved wood headboard behind him and a portion of the same wooden window upper right; the modest pale golden star is to the left of the crescent moon outside, same leafy cream curtains. Warm bedside lamp from lower left gives a much softer subdued glow. Same watercolor pigments and paper texture, low saturation, cozy dusty blue shadows. Keep paws anatomically simple and the carrot toy consistent, no new characters or props. No text, labels, border, collage or watermark. Vertical 9:16.

第100集 · 森林的晚安灯　保留故事核后重写

讨论方向，未批准：作为阶段收束，走访此前真实出现的不同地点与朋友，每处完成一个轻小收尾，最后用森林全景道晚安；不再只看皮皮窗户。
